<a class="button-arrow-prev">Előző</a>
<a class="button-arrow-next">Következő</a>

<script src="https://code.jquery.com/jquery-1.11.1.min.js"></script>
<script>

                var slideLock = false;

                function displaySlide($activeArticle, $otherArticle, $activeTab, $otherTab){

                    if(false == slideLock){
                        slideLock = true;
                        $otherArticle.css({
                            'opacity': '1'
                        });

                        $activeArticle.animate(
                            {
                                'opacity': 0
                            },
                            500,
                            function(){
                                $activeArticle.removeClass('active');
                                $otherArticle.addClass('active');
                                slideLock = false;
                            }
                        );

                        $activeTab.removeClass('active');
                        $otherTab.addClass('active');
                    }
                }

                function changeSlide(action){
                    var $activeArticle = $('.top-story').find('article.active');
                    var $activeTab = $('.top-story').find('.releated-articles li.active');

                    var $otherArticle = null;
                    var $otherTab = null;
                    if('prev' == action){
                        $otherArticle = $activeArticle.prev('article');
                        $otherTab = $activeTab.prev('li');;
                    }
                    else if('next' == action){
                        $otherArticle = $activeArticle.next('article');
                        $otherTab = $activeTab.next('li');;
                    }

                    if("undefined" == typeof $otherArticle || !$otherArticle.length){

                        if(action == 'prev'){
                            $otherArticle = $('.top-story').find('article').last();
                            $otherTab = $('.top-story').find('.releated-articles li').last();
                        }
                        else if(action == 'next'){
                            $otherArticle = $('.top-story').find('article').first();
                            $otherTab = $('.top-story').find('.releated-articles li').first();
                        }
                    }

                    displaySlide($activeArticle, $otherArticle, $activeTab, $otherTab);
                }

                $(document).ready(function(){

                    // Rotate top story articles
                    var rotate = setInterval(
                        function(){
                            changeSlide('next');
                        },
                        5000
                    );

                    // Show/hide pagers
                    $('.top-story').hover(
                        function(){
                            $(this).find('.slideshow-pager').show();
                        },
                        function(){
                            $(this).find('.slideshow-pager').hide();
                        }
                    );

                    // Change slide by click on tabs
/*
                    $('.top-story .releated-articles a').click(function(e){
                        clearInterval(rotate);
                        var id = parseInt($(this).closest('li').data('id'));
                        var $activeArticle = $('.top-story').find('article.active');
                        var $activeTab = $('.top-story').find('.releated-articles li.active');

                        if(id > 0 && id != $activeTab.data('id')){
                            var $otherTab = $(this).closest('li');
                            var $otherArticle = $('.top-story').find('article[data-id="'+ id +'"]');

                            displaySlide($activeArticle, $otherArticle, $activeTab, $otherTab);
                        }
                        e.preventDefault();
                    });
*/

                    $('.top-story .releated-articles a').hover(function(e){
                        clearInterval(rotate);
                        var id = parseInt($(this).closest('li').data('id'));
                        var $activeArticle = $('.top-story').find('article.active');
                        var $activeTab = $('.top-story').find('.releated-articles li.active');

                        if(id > 0 && id != $activeTab.data('id')){
                            var $otherTab = $(this).closest('li');
                            var $otherArticle = $('.top-story').find('article[data-id="'+ id +'"]');

                            displaySlide($activeArticle, $otherArticle, $activeTab, $otherTab);
                        }
                        e.preventDefault();
                    });

                    $(".slideshow-pager a.button-arrow-prev").click(function(e){
                            changeSlide('prev');
                            clearInterval(rotate);
                            e.preventDefault();
                    });

                    $(".slideshow-pager a.button-arrow-next").click(function(e){
                        changeSlide('next');
                        clearInterval(rotate);
                        e.preventDefault();
                    });

                });
            </script>

<h2 class="rovat-tag"><a href="/kismagyarorszag">Kis-Magyarország</a></h2>
<em class="bgfa"></em>
<h3 class=""><a href="/kismagyarorszag/6-milliard-forint-kozpenz-a-magankezben-levo-focisulinak-az-illes-akademianak-epul-az-uj-utanpotlas-centrum-123937">6 milliárd forint közpénz a magánkézben lévő focisulinak</a></h3>
<em class="bgfa"></em>
<p>Az Illés Akadémia fogja üzemeltetni a mellette épülő Nyugat-Dunántúli Utánpótlás Nevelési Központot.</p>
